<p>In version control software, a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Changeset" title="Changeset">changeset</a> (also known as commit<sup about="#mwt115" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Changeset_gitglossary-changeset_33-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Changeset gitglossary-changeset&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-Changeset_gitglossary-changeset-33&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Changeset_gitglossary-changeset-33" id="mwAZI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZQ">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZU">]</span></span></a></sup> and revision<sup about="#mwt117" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-34" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-34&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-34" id="mwAZY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZg">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZk">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt119" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-35" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-35&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-35" id="mwAZo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZw">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZ0">]</span></span></a></sup>) is a set of alterations packaged together, along with meta-information about the alterations.  A changeset describes the exact differences between two successive versions in the version control system's repository of changes.  Changesets are typically treated as an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atomicity_(database_systems)" title="Atomicity (database systems)">atomic</a> unit, an indivisible set, by version control systems.  This is one <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Synchronization_model" title="Synchronization model">synchronization model</a>.<sup about="#mwt122" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-36" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-36&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-36" id="mwAZ4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZ8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaA">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt126" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-37" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-37&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-37" id="mwAaI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAaM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaQ">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_excerpts">
</div></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="34" id="mwAaY" aria-labelledby="Commit_(verb)"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Commit_(verb)"><span id="Commit_.28verb.29" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>Commit (verb)</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=34" title="Edit section: Commit (verb)"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt129" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAac" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Commit (revision control)&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt130" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt129" id="mwAag">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commit_(revision_control)" title="Commit (revision control)" class="mw-redirect">Commit (revision control)</a></div>

<p id="mwAak">To <i id="mwAao"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commit_(revision_control)" title="Commit (revision control)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAas">commit</a></i> (<i id="mwAaw">check in</i>, <i id="mwAa0">ci</i> or, more rarely, <i id="mwAa4">install</i>, <i id="mwAa8">submit</i> or <i id="mwAbA">record</i>) is to write or merge the changes made in the working copy back to the repository. A commit contains metadata, typically the author information and a commit message that describes the change.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="35" id="mwAbE" aria-labelledby="Commit_message"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Commit_message">Commit message</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=35" title="Edit section: Commit message"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAbI">A short note, written by the developer, stored with the commit, which describes the commit.  Ideally, it records why the modification was made, a description of the modification's effect or purpose, and non-obvious aspects of how the change works.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="36" id="mwAbM" aria-labelledby="Conflict"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Conflict">Conflict</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=36" title="Edit section: Conflict"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAbQ">A conflict occurs when different parties make changes to the same document, and the system is unable to reconcile the changes.  A user must <i id="mwAbU">resolve</i> the conflict by combining the changes, or by selecting one change in favour of the other.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="37" id="mwAbY" aria-labelledby="Delta_compression"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Delta_compression">Delta compression</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=37" title="Edit section: Delta compression"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt131" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAbc" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Delta compression&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt132" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt131" id="mwAbg">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delta_compression" title="Delta compression" class="mw-redirect">Delta compression</a></div>

<p id="mwAbk">Most revision control software uses <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delta_compression" title="Delta compression" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAbo">delta compression</a>, which retains only the differences between successive versions of files. This allows for more efficient storage of many different versions of files.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="38" id="mwAbs" aria-labelledby="Dynamic_stream"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Dynamic_stream">Dynamic stream</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=38" title="Edit section: Dynamic stream"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAbw">A stream in which some or all file versions are mirrors of the parent stream's versions.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="39" id="mwAb0" aria-labelledby="Export"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Export">Export</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=39" title="Edit section: Export"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAb4"><i id="mwAb8">Exporting</i> is the act of obtaining the files from the repository. It is similar to <i id="mwAcA">checking out</i> except that it creates a clean directory tree without the version-control metadata used in a working copy. This is often used prior to publishing the contents, for example.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="40" id="mwAcE" aria-labelledby="Fetch"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Fetch">Fetch</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=40" title="Edit section: Fetch"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAcI">See <i id="mwAcM">pull</i>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="41" id="mwAcQ" aria-labelledby="Forward_integration"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Forward_integration">Forward integration</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=41" title="Edit section: Forward integration"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAcU">The process of merging changes made in the main <i id="mwAcY">trunk</i> into a development (feature or team) branch.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="42" id="mwAcc" aria-labelledby="Head"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Head">Head</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=42" title="Edit section: Head"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAcg">Also sometimes called <i id="mwAck">tip</i>, this refers to the most recent commit, either to the trunk or to a branch. The trunk and each branch have their own head, though HEAD is sometimes loosely used to refer to the trunk.<sup about="#mwt135" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-38" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-38&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-38" id="mwAco"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcw">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAc0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="43" id="mwAc4" aria-labelledby="Import"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Import">Import</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=43" title="Edit section: Import"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAc8"><i id="mwAdA">Importing</i> is the act of copying a local directory tree (that is not currently a working copy) into the repository for the first time.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="44" id="mwAdE" aria-labelledby="Initialize"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Initialize">Initialize</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=44" title="Edit section: Initialize"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAdI">To create a new, empty repository.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="45" id="mwAdM" aria-labelledby="Interleaved_deltas"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Interleaved_deltas">Interleaved deltas</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=45" title="Edit section: Interleaved deltas"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAdQ">Some revision control software uses <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Interleaved_deltas" title="Interleaved deltas" id="mwAdU">Interleaved deltas</a>, a method that allows storing the history of text based files in a more efficient way than by using <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Delta_compression" title="Delta compression" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAdY">Delta compression</a>.</p>
 
</section><section data-mw-section-id="46" id="mwAdc" aria-labelledby="Label"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Label">Label</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=46" title="Edit section: Label"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAdg">See <i id="mwAdk">tag</i>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="47" id="mwAdo" aria-labelledby="Locking"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Locking">Locking</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=47" title="Edit section: Locking"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt136" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAds" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;File locking&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt137" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt136" id="mwAdw">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File_locking" title="File locking">File locking</a></div>

<p id="mwAd0">When a developer <i id="mwAd4"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File_locking" title="File locking" id="mwAd8">locks</a></i> a file, no one else can update that file until it is unlocked. Locking can be supported by the version control system, or via informal communications between developers (aka <i id="mwAeA">social locking</i>).</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="48" id="mwAeE" aria-labelledby="Mainline"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Mainline">Mainline</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=48" title="Edit section: Mainline"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAeI">Similar to <i id="mwAeM">trunk</i>, but there can be a mainline for each branch.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="49" id="mwAeQ" aria-labelledby="Merge"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Merge">Merge</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=49" title="Edit section: Merge"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt138" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAeU" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Merge (revision control)&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt139" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt138" id="mwAeY">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Merge_(revision_control)" title="Merge (revision control)" class="mw-redirect">Merge (revision control)</a></div>

<p id="mwAec">A <i id="mwAeg">merge</i> or <i id="mwAek">integration</i> is an operation in which two sets of changes are applied to a file or set of files. Some sample scenarios are as follows:</p>
<ul id="mwAeo"><li id="mwAes">A user, working on a set of files, <i id="mwAew">updates</i> or <i id="mwAe0">syncs</i> their working copy with changes made, and checked into the repository, by other users.<sup about="#mwt140" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve_1.5:_SVN_tour_cycle_resolve]_39-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve 1.5: SVN tour cycle resolve]&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve_1.5:_SVN_tour_cycle_resolve]-39&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Sfn &quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Sfn&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot; Collins-Sussman &quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot; Fitzpatrick &quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot; Pilato &quot;},&quot;4&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot; 2004 &quot;},&quot;loc&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;[http://svnbook.red-bean.com/en/1.5/svn.tour.cycle.html#svn.tour.cycle.resolve  1.5: SVN tour cycle resolve]&quot;},&quot;ps&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;: ‘The G stands for merGed, which means that the file had local changes to begin with, but the changes coming from the repository didn't overlap with the local changes.’&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve_1.5:_SVN_tour_cycle_resolve]-39" id="mwAe4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAe8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfA">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfE">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAfI">A user tries to <i id="mwAfM">check in</i> files that have been updated by others since the files were <i id="mwAfQ">checked out</i>, and the <i id="mwAfU">revision control software</i> automatically merges the files (typically, after prompting the user if it should proceed with the automatic merge, and in some cases only doing so if the merge can be clearly and reasonably resolved).</li>
<li id="mwAfY">A <i id="mwAfc">branch</i> is created, the code in the files is independently edited, and the updated branch is later incorporated into a single, unified <i id="mwAfg">trunk</i>.</li>
<li id="mwAfk">A set of files is <i id="mwAfo">branched</i>, a problem that existed before the branching is fixed in one branch, and the fix is then merged into the other branch. (This type of selective merge is sometimes known as a <i id="mwAfs">cherry pick</i> to distinguish it from the complete merge in the previous case.)</li></ul>
</section><section data-mw-section-id="50" id="mwAfw" aria-labelledby="Promote"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Promote">Promote</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=50" title="Edit section: Promote"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAf0">The act of copying file content from a less controlled location into a more controlled location. For example, from a user's workspace into a repository, or from a stream to its parent.<sup about="#mwt145" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-40" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-40&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-40" id="mwAf4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAf8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgA">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgE">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
</section><section data-mw-section-id="51" id="mwAgI" aria-labelledby="Pull,_push"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pull,_push"><span id="Pull.2C_push" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>Pull, push</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=51" title="Edit section: Pull, push"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAgM">Copy revisions from one repository into another. <i id="mwAgQ">Pull</i> is initiated by the receiving repository, while <i id="mwAgU">push</i> is initiated by the source. <i id="mwAgY">Fetch</i> is sometimes used as a synonym for <i id="mwAgc">pull</i>, or to mean a <i id="mwAgg">pull</i> followed by an <i id="mwAgk">update</i>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="52" id="mwAgo" aria-labelledby="Pull_request"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pull_request">Pull request</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=52" title="Edit section: Pull request"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="excerpt-block" about="#mwt146" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAgs" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Excerpt&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Excerpt&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Distributed version control&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Pull requests&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="mw-empty-elt"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1353838603" about="#mwt147" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Excerpt/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt148" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable dablink excerpt-hat selfref">This section is an excerpt from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Distributed_version_control#Pull_requests" title="Distributed version control">Distributed version control § Pull requests</a>.<span class="mw-editsection-like plainlinks"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Distributed_version_control&amp;action=edit" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Distributed_version_control&amp;action=edit">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div><div class="excerpt">
<p>Contributions to a source code repository that uses a distributed version control system are commonly made by means of a pull request, also known as a merge request.<sup about="#mwt152" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Distributed_version_control_gitlab-merge-req_41-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Distributed version control gitlab-merge-req&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-Distributed_version_control_gitlab-merge-req-41&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Distributed_version_control_gitlab-merge-req-41" id="mwAgw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAg0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAg4">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAg8">]</span></span></a></sup> The contributor requests that the project maintainer <i>pull</i> the source code change, hence the name "pull request". The maintainer has to <i>merge</i> the pull request if the contribution should become part of the source base.<sup about="#mwt157" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Distributed_version_control_ossw_42-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Distributed version control ossw&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-Distributed_version_control_ossw-42&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Distributed_version_control_ossw-42" id="mwAhA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAhE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhI">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p>The developer creates a pull request to notify maintainers of a new change; a comment thread is associated with each pull request. This allows for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Code_review" title="Code review">focused discussion of code changes</a>. Submitted pull requests are visible to anyone with repository access. A pull request can be accepted or rejected by maintainers.<sup about="#mwt162" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-43" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-43&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-43" id="mwAhQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAhU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhY">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p>Once the pull request is reviewed and approved, it is merged into the repository. Depending on the established workflow, the code may need to be tested before being included into official release. Therefore, some projects contain a special branch for merging untested pull requests.<sup about="#mwt165" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Distributed_version_control_ossw_42-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Distributed version control ossw&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Distributed_version_control_ossw-42" id="mwAhg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAhk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAho">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhs">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt169" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-44" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-44&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-44" id="mwAhw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAh0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAh4">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAh8">]</span></span></a></sup> Other projects run an automated test suite on every pull request, using a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Continuous_integration" title="Continuous integration">continuous integration</a> tool, and the reviewer checks that any new code has appropriate test coverage.</p>
<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_excerpts">
</div></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="53" id="mwAiA" aria-labelledby="Repository"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Repository">Repository</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=53" title="Edit section: Repository"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="excerpt-block" about="#mwt172" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAiE" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Excerpt&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Excerpt&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Repository (version control)&quot;},&quot;paragraph&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;1&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="mw-empty-elt"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1353838603" about="#mwt173" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Excerpt/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt174" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable dablink excerpt-hat selfref">This section is an excerpt from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Repository_(version_control)" title="Repository (version control)">Repository (version control)</a>.<span class="mw-editsection-like plainlinks"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Repository_(version_control)&amp;action=edit" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Repository_(version_control)&amp;action=edit">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div><div class="excerpt">
<p>In version control systems, a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Repository_(version_control)" title="Repository (version control)">repository</a> is a data structure that stores either <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metadata" title="Metadata">metadata</a> or metadata and files for a set of files or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Directory_structure" title="Directory structure">directory structure</a>.<sup about="#mwt177" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-45" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-45&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-45" id="mwAiI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAiM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAiQ">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAiU">]</span></span></a></sup> Depending on whether the version control system in use is distributed, like <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Git" title="Git">Git</a> or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mercurial" title="Mercurial">Mercurial</a>, or centralized, like <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apache_Subversion" title="Apache Subversion">Subversion</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Concurrent_Versions_System" title="Concurrent Versions System">CVS</a>, or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Perforce" title="Perforce">Perforce</a>, the whole set of information in the repository may be duplicated on every user's system or may be maintained on a single <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Server_(computing)" title="Server (computing)">server</a>.<sup about="#mwt182" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Repository_(version_control)_Ernst-2018_46-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Repository (version control) Ernst-2018&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-Repository_(version_control)_Ernst-2018-46&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Repository_(version_control)_Ernst-2018-46" id="mwAiY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAic"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAig">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAik">]</span></span></a></sup> Some of the metadata that a repository contains includes, among other things, a historical record of changes in the repository, a set of commit objects, and a set of references to commit objects, called <i>heads</i>.</p>
<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_excerpts">
</div></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="54" id="mwAio" aria-labelledby="Resolve"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Resolve">Resolve</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=54" title="Edit section: Resolve"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAis">The act of user intervention to address a conflict between different changes to the same document.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="55" id="mwAiw" aria-labelledby="Reverse_integration"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Reverse_integration">Reverse integration</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=55" title="Edit section: Reverse integration"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAi0">The process of merging different team branches into the main trunk of the versioning system.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="56" id="mwAi4" aria-labelledby="Reversion"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Reversion">Reversion</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=56" title="Edit section: Reversion"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAi8">The abandonment of one or more recent changes in favor of a return to a previous version of the material at hand. A revert may be done for many reasons, including: fixing errors introduced by previous edits; restoring the material to a state that was not contentious until new disputes can be resolved; undoing <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scope_creep" title="Scope creep" id="mwAjA">scope creep</a>; and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Regression_testing" title="Regression testing" id="mwAjE">regression testing</a>.</p>

<span about="#mwt185" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAjI" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;DEFAULTSORT:Version control&quot;,&quot;function&quot;:&quot;defaultsort&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"></span>
<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Software_development_process" id="mwAjM">
</section><section data-mw-section-id="57" id="mwAjQ" aria-labelledby="Revision_and_version"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Revision_and_version">Revision and version</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=57" title="Edit section: Revision and version"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAjU">A <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Software_versioning" title="Software versioning" id="mwAjY">version</a> is any change in form. In SVK, a Revision is the state at a point in time of the entire tree in the repository.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="58" id="mwAjc" aria-labelledby="Share"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Share">Share</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=58" title="Edit section: Share"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAjg">The act of making one file or folder available in multiple branches at the same time. When a shared file is changed in one branch, it is changed in other branches.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="59" id="mwAjk" aria-labelledby="Stream"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Stream">Stream</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=59" title="Edit section: Stream"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAjo">A container for branched files that has a known relationship to other such containers. Streams form a hierarchy; each stream can inherit various properties (like versions, namespace, workflow rules, subscribers, etc.) from its parent stream.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="60" id="mwAjs" aria-labelledby="Tag"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Tag">Tag</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=60" title="Edit section: Tag"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt186" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAjw" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Revision tag&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt187" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt186" id="mwAj0">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Revision_tag" title="Revision tag">Revision tag</a></div>
<p id="mwAj4">A <i id="mwAj8"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Revision_tag" title="Revision tag" id="mwAkA">tag</a></i> or <i id="mwAkE">label</i> refers to an important snapshot in time, consistent across many files.  These files at that point may all be tagged with a user-friendly, meaningful name or revision number.  See <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="#Baselines,_labels_and_tags" class="mw-selflink-fragment" id="mwAkI">baselines, labels and tags</a>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="61" id="mwAkM" aria-labelledby="Trunk"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Trunk">Trunk</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=61" title="Edit section: Trunk"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt188" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAkQ" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Trunk (software)&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt189" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt188" id="mwAkU">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trunk_(software)" title="Trunk (software)" class="mw-redirect">Trunk (software)</a></div>

<p id="mwAkY">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trunk_(software)" title="Trunk (software)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAkc">trunk</a> is the unique line of development that is not a branch (sometimes also called Baseline, Mainline or Master<sup about="#mwt192" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-47" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-47&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-47" id="mwAkg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAkk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAko">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAks">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt195" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-48" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-48&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-48" id="mwAkw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAk0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAk4">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAk8">]</span></span></a></sup>)</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="62" id="mwAlA" aria-labelledby="Update"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Update">Update</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=62" title="Edit section: Update"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAlE">An <i id="mwAlI">update</i> (or <i id="mwAlM">sync</i>, but <i id="mwAlQ">sync</i> can also mean a combined <i id="mwAlU">push</i> and <i id="mwAlY">pull</i>) merges changes made in the repository (by other people, for example) into the local <i id="mwAlc">working copy</i>.  <i id="mwAlg">Update</i> is also the term used by some CM tools (CM+, PLS, SMS) for the change package concept (see <i id="mwAlk">changelist</i>). Synonymous with <i id="mwAlo">checkout</i> in revision control systems that require each repository to have exactly one working copy (common in distributed systems)</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="63" id="mwAls" aria-labelledby="Unlocking"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Unlocking">Unlocking</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=63" title="Edit section: Unlocking"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAlw">Releasing a lock.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="64" id="mwAl0" aria-labelledby="Working_copy"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Working_copy">Working copy</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=64" title="Edit section: Working copy"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAl4">The <i id="mwAl8">working copy</i> is the local copy of files from a repository, at a specific time or revision. All work done to the files in a repository is initially done on a working copy, hence the name. Conceptually, it is a <i id="mwAmA"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sandbox_(software_development)" title="Sandbox (software development)" id="mwAmE">sandbox</a></i>.</p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="65" id="mwAmI" aria-labelledby="See_also"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="See_also">See also</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=65" title="Edit section: See also"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="66" id="mwAmU" aria-labelledby="Notes"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Notes">Notes</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Version_control&amp;action=edit&amp;section=66" title="Edit section: Notes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt198" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAmY" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Reflist&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Reflist&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;note&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1327269900" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt199" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Reflist/styles.css&quot;}}">.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-2{column-width:30em}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-3{column-width:25em}body.skin-vector-2022 .mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-2{column-width:27em}body.skin-vector-2022 .mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-3{column-width:22.5em}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=upper-alpha]{list-style-type:upper-alpha}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=upper-roman]{list-style-type:upper-roman}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-alpha]{list-style-type:lower-alpha}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-greek]{list-style-type:lower-greek}.mw-parser-output .references[data-mw-group=lower-roman]{list-style-type:lower-roman}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-upper-alpha .references{list-style-type:upper-alpha}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-upper-roman .references{list-style-type:upper-roman}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-alpha .references{list-style-type:lower-alpha}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-greek .references{list-style-type:lower-greek}.mw-parser-output div.reflist-liststyle-lower-roman .references{list-style-type:lower-roman}</style></span><div about="#mwt198" id="mwAmc">
<div class="mw-references-wrap" typeof="mw:Extension/references" about="#mwt200" id="mwAmg" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;references&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;group&quot;:&quot;note&quot;,&quot;responsive&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"><ol class="mw-references references" data-mw-group="note" id="mwAmk"><li about="#cite_note-10" id="cite_note-10" data-mw-footnote-number="1"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwAmo"><a href="#cite_ref-10" data-mw-group="note" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwAms" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwAmw">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-10" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="note">In this case, edit buffers are a secondary form of working copy, and not referred to as such.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-11" id="cite_note-11" data-mw-footnote-number="2"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwAm0"><a href="#cite_ref-11" data-mw-group="note" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwAm4" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwAm8">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-11" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="note">In principle two revisions can have identical timestamp, and thus cannot be ordered on a line. This is generally the case for separate repositories, though is also possible for simultaneous changes to several branches in a single repository. In these cases, the revisions can be thought of as a set of separate lines, one per repository or branch (or branch within a repository).</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-12" id="cite_note-12" data-mw-footnote-number="3"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwAnA"><a href="#cite_ref-12" data-mw-group="note" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwAnE" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwAnI">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-12" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="note">The revision or repository "tree" should not be confused with the directory tree of files in a working copy.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-13" id="cite_note-13" data-mw-footnote-number="4"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwAnM"><a href="#cite_ref-13" data-mw-group="note" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwAnQ" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwAnU">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-13" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="note">If a new branch is based on HEAD, then topologically HEAD is no longer a tip, since it has a child.</span></li>
<li about="#cite_note-14" id="cite_note-14" data-mw-footnote-number="5"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwAnY"><a href="#cite_ref-14" data-mw-group="note" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwAnc" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwAng">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-14" class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="note">"Mainline" can also refer to the main path in a separate branch.</span></li>
</ol></div></div>

<li about="#cite_note-25" id="cite_note-25" data-mw-footnote-number="20"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwA2U"><a href="#cite_ref-25" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwA2Y" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwA2c">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-25" class="mw-reference-text reference-text"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333433106" about="#mwt84" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwA2g" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Citation/CS1/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;cite web &quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Cite_web&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;url&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;https://reqtest.com/requirements-blog/what-are-benefits-of-version-control/&quot;},&quot;author&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;ReQtest&quot;},&quot;title&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;What Are The Benefits Of Version Control?&quot;},&quot;date&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2020-10-26&quot;},&quot;access-date&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2022-11-21&quot;},&quot;quote&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;The history of the document provides invaluable information about the author and the date of editing. The ability to identify the author of the document enables the current team to link the documents to specific contributors. This, in turn, enables the current team to uncover patterns that can help with fixing bugs. This will help to improve the overall functionality of the software.&quot;},&quot;archive-date&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2022-11-22&quot;},&quot;archive-url&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;https://web.archive.org/web/20221122023907/https://reqtest.com/requirements-blog/what-are-benefits-of-version-control/&quot;},&quot;url-status&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;live&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><cite id="CITEREFReQtest2020" class="citation web cs1" about="#mwt84">ReQtest (2020-10-26). <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://reqtest.com/requirements-blog/what-are-benefits-of-version-control/" class="external text" id="mwA2k">"What Are The Benefits Of Version Control?"</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://web.archive.org/web/20221122023907/https://reqtest.com/requirements-blog/what-are-benefits-of-version-control/" class="external text" id="mwA2o">Archived</a> from the original on 2022-11-22<span class="reference-accessdate" id="mwA2s">. Retrieved <span class="nowrap" id="mwA2w">2022-11-21</span></span>. <q id="mwA20">The history of the document provides invaluable information about the author and the date of editing. It also gives on the purpose of the changes made. It will have an impact on the developer that works on the latest version as it will help to solve problems experienced in earlier versions. The ability to identify the author of the document enables the current team to link the documents to specific contributors. This, in turn, enables the current team to uncover patterns that can help with fixing bugs. This will help to improve the overall functionality of the software.</q></cite><span title="ctx_ver=Z39.88-2004&amp;rft_val_fmt=info%3Aofi%2Ffmt%3Akev%3Amtx%3Abook&amp;rft.genre=unknown&amp;rft.btitle=What+Are+The+Benefits+Of+Version+Control%3F&amp;rft.date=2020-10-26&amp;rft.au=ReQtest&amp;rft_id=https%3A%2F%2Freqtest.com%2Frequirements-blog%2Fwhat-are-benefits-of-version-control%2F&amp;rfr_id=info%3Asid%2Fen.wikipedia.org%3AVersion+control" class="Z3988" about="#mwt84" id="mwA24"></span></span></li>

<li about="#cite_note-29" id="cite_note-29" data-mw-footnote-number="24"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwA48"><a href="#cite_ref-29" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwA5A" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwA5E">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-29" class="mw-reference-text reference-text"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333433106" about="#mwt96" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwA5I" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Citation/CS1/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;cite web &quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Cite_web&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;url&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;https://www.seguetech.com/a-review-of-software-version-control-systems-benefits-and-why-it-matters/&quot;},&quot;title&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;A Review of Software Version Control: Systems, Benefits, and Why it Matters&quot;},&quot;author&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Irma Azarian&quot;},&quot;date&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2023-06-14&quot;},&quot;access-date&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2022-11-18&quot;},&quot;quote&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Versioning is a priceless process, especially when you have multiple developers working on a single application, because it allows them to easily share files. Without version control, developers will eventually step on each other’s toes and overwrite code changes that someone else may have completed without even realizing it. Using these systems allows you to check files out for modifications, then, during check-in, if the files have been changed by another user, you will be alerted and allowed to merge them.&quot;},&quot;archive-date&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2024-09-26&quot;},&quot;archive-url&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;https://web.archive.org/web/20240926165505/https://www.seguetech.com/a-review-of-software-version-control-systems-benefits-and-why-it-matters/&quot;},&quot;url-status&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;live&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><cite id="mwA5M" class="citation web cs1" about="#mwt96" data-x-id="CITEREFIrma_Azarian2023">Irma Azarian (2023-06-14). <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.seguetech.com/a-review-of-software-version-control-systems-benefits-and-why-it-matters/" class="external text" id="mwA5Q">"A Review of Software Version Control: Systems, Benefits, and Why it Matters"</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://web.archive.org/web/20240926165505/https://www.seguetech.com/a-review-of-software-version-control-systems-benefits-and-why-it-matters/" class="external text" id="mwA5U">Archived</a> from the original on 2024-09-26<span class="reference-accessdate" id="mwA5Y">. Retrieved <span class="nowrap" id="mwA5c">2022-11-18</span></span>. <q id="mwA5g">Versioning is a priceless process, especially when you have multiple developers working on a single application, because it allows them to easily share files. Without version control, developers will eventually step on each other's toes and overwrite code changes that someone else may have completed without even realizing it. Using these systems allows you to check files out for modifications, then, during check-in, if the files have been changed by another user, you will be alerted and allowed to merge them.</q></cite><span title="ctx_ver=Z39.88-2004&amp;rft_val_fmt=info%3Aofi%2Ffmt%3Akev%3Amtx%3Abook&amp;rft.genre=unknown&amp;rft.btitle=A+Review+of+Software+Version+Control%3A+Systems%2C+Benefits%2C+and+Why+it+Matters&amp;rft.date=2023-06-14&amp;rft.au=Irma+Azarian&amp;rft_id=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.seguetech.com%2Fa-review-of-software-version-control-systems-benefits-and-why-it-matters%2F&amp;rfr_id=info%3Asid%2Fen.wikipedia.org%3AVersion+control" class="Z3988" about="#mwt96" id="mwA5k"></span></span></li>

<li about="#cite_note-FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve_1.5:_SVN_tour_cycle_resolve]-39" id="cite_note-FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve_1.5:_SVN_tour_cycle_resolve]-39" data-mw-footnote-number="34"><span class="mw-cite-backlink" id="mwA-g"><a href="#cite_ref-FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve_1.5:_SVN_tour_cycle_resolve]_39-0" rel="mw:referencedBy" id="mwA-k" aria-label="Jump up" title="Jump up"><span class="mw-linkback-text" id="mwA-o">↑</span></a></span> <span id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-FOOTNOTECollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004[httpsvnbookred-beancomen15svntourcyclehtmlsvntourcycleresolve_1.5:_SVN_tour_cycle_resolve]-39" class="mw-reference-text reference-text"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="#CITEREFCollins-SussmanFitzpatrickPilato2004" class="mw-selflink-fragment" id="mwA-s">Collins-Sussman, Fitzpatrick <span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwA-w">&amp;</span> Pilato 2004</a>, <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="http://svnbook.red-bean.com/en/1.5/svn.tour.cycle.html#svn.tour.cycle.resolve" class="external text" id="mwA-0">1.5: SVN tour cycle resolve</a>: ‘The G stands for merGed, which means that the file had local changes to begin with, but the changes coming from the repository didn't overlap with the local changes.’</span></li>
